  vision2030_contemporary_site\templates\briefing.html:713:      <p class="sec-sub" id="corr-sub">Reading across 55 facilities under AdventHealth System, this looks for Vision 2030 metrics whose facility-level scores rise and fall together — a signal they may share a common driver or trade off against one another.</p>
> vision2030_contemporary_site\templates\briefing.html:714:      <div class="callout" id="corr-callout" style="background:#EAF4F9;border:1px solid #BEDDEC;border-left:4px solid var(--primary);border-radius:8px;padding:16px 20px;margin-bottom:24px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.55;color:var(--ink-soft);"><b>How this is computed.</b> Each metric’s status is scored on the workbook scale (at goal = 100, approaching = 73, below = 30) and a Pearson correlation (r) is run for every pair of metrics across the 55 facilities. Only pairs where both metrics vary and share at least five reporting facilities appear; |r| ≥ 0.4 is flagged notable (≥ 0.7 strong, ≥ 0.5 moderate). <b>Correlation is not causation</b> — these are prompts to investigate, not proof.</div>

> vision2030_contemporary_site\templates\insights.html:714:      <div class="callout" id="corr-callout" style="background:#EAF4F9;border:1px solid #BEDDEC;border-left:4px solid var(--primary);border-radius:8px;padding:16px 20px;margin-bottom:24px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.55;color:var(--ink-soft);"><b>How this is computed.</b> Each metric’s status is scored on the workbook scale (at goal = 100, approaching = 73, below = 30) and a Pearson correlation (r) is run for every pair of metrics across the 55 facilities. Only pairs where both metrics vary and share at least five reporting facilities appear; |r| ≥ 0.4 is flagged notable (≥ 0.7 strong, ≥ 0.5 moderate). <b>Correlation is not causation</b> — these are prompts to investigate, not proof.</div>

> vision2030_contemporary_site\templates\data_explorer.html:714:      <div class="callout" id="corr-callout" style="background:#EAF4F9;border:1px solid #BEDDEC;border-left:4px solid var(--primary);border-radius:8px;padding:16px 20px;margin-bottom:24px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.55;color:var(--ink-soft);"><b>How this is computed.</b> Each metric’s status is scored on the workbook scale (at goal = 100, approaching = 73, below = 30) and a Pearson correlation (r) is run for every pair of metrics across the 55 facilities.
